Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The molecular variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 445 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 116 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the molecular space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 445 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 116. QED.
